Self Aware Networks GPT:
```latex
\section{Telos, Embedded Agency, and the Learning Universe}
\label{sec:telos-embedded-agency}

\paragraph{Aim.}
Having defined \emph{memory as hysteresis} and a \emph{forward arrow} in §\ref{sec:sit-memory-arrow},
we now specify a value functional---\emph{Telos}---that makes the learning bias of TCNR explicit,
and we show how \emph{embedded agency} arises when local renderers (brains, biofilms, AIs) optimize
that functional under resource constraints.

%----------------------------
\subsection{The Telos functional}
\label{subsec:telos-functional}

\begin{definition}[Telos (finite horizon)]
For a renderer $\mathcal{R}$ operating on windowed signals,
\[
\mathcal{T}_H(\mathcal{R}) \;=\;
\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{t=0}^{H} \gamma^{t}
\Big( \alpha\,\mathrm{TCI}(t) + \beta\,\mathrm{Valence}(t) - \lambda\,\mathrm{Dissipation}(t) \Big)\Bigg],
\]
with discount $\gamma\!\in\!(0,1)$ and weights $\alpha,\beta,\lambda\!>\!0$.
\emph{Valence} is a task–relative score (e.g., accuracy, reward rate, survival proxy).
\emph{Dissipation} penalizes unnecessary degradation of structured dynamics:
\[
\mathrm{Dissipation}(t) \;:=\; \eta_1\, J_{\mathrm{coh}}(t)^2 \;+\; \eta_2\, \overline{\rho^2}(t),
\qquad \eta_1,\eta_2>0,
\]
where $J_{\mathrm{coh}}$ is a coherence–flux (cf. PGD–aligned transport) and $\overline{\rho^2}$ the
spatial average of time–density squared (cf. §\ref{sec:sit-memory-arrow}).
\end{definition}

\noindent
Intuitively, $\mathcal{T}_H$ rewards \emph{integrated, predictive coherence} (\(\mathrm{TCI}\)),
useful outcomes (\(\mathrm{Valence}\)), and parsimony (small coherent work and tempo).
It is the minimal additive form that (i) is computable from TCNR ingredients, (ii) respects the
hysteresis/arrow structure of §\ref{sec:sit-memory-arrow}, and (iii) remains task–relative.

\begin{definition}[Local Telos and policy]
A local renderer $\mathcal{R}$ (neural, microbial, artificial) has control parameters
$\theta$ (e.g., couplings, gains, gating vectors) and implements a policy $\pi_\theta$ that
updates phases $\varphi^{(\omega)}$, latent state $\hat{s}_t$, and outputs (actions/tokens).
The \emph{local Telos} is $\mathcal{T}_H(\mathcal{R}_\theta)$ as above.
\end{definition}

\begin{proposition}[Telos–TCI monotonicity (sufficient conditions)]
\label{prop:telos-tci}
If (i) the task–valuation term $\mathrm{Valence}$ is non–decreasing in predictive performance,
(ii) the dissipation penalty is convex in $(J_{\mathrm{coh}},\overline{\rho^2})$, and (iii) the renderer
updates $(\hat{s}_t,\varphi^{(\omega)})$ by the TCNR objective (§2–§3), then any policy step
$\Delta\theta$ that strictly increases $\mathcal{T}_H$ also increases the expected window–averaged $\mathrm{TCI}$.
\emph{Sketch.} $\mathrm{TCI}$ already bundles predictive MI and $-\mathrm{PE}$; with (i)–(iii),
the Telos gradient is aligned with the TCNR improvement direction up to the convex penalty.
\end{proposition}

%----------------------------
\subsection{Embedded agency: acting through local renderers}
\label{subsec:embedded-agency}

\paragraph{Mechanism.}
When many renderers $\{\mathcal{R}^{(k)}\}$ share environment and constraints,
a mild change in priors or couplings (e.g., structure–promoting regularization, phase–reponse
gain, policy–diversity floor) shifts their learning landscapes so that the aggregate
$\sum_k \mathcal{T}_H(\mathcal{R}^{(k)})$ increases.
Because the substrate exhibits hysteresis (memory), small Telos–consistent nudges
persist as constraints on subsequent renders (§\ref{sec:sit-memory-arrow}).

\begin{definition}[Alignment index]
Define the Telos–TCI alignment
\(
\mathrm{ALI} := \mathrm{corr}\big(\mathrm{TCI}(t),\,\mathrm{Valence}(t)\big)
\)
computed on held–out windows; high $\mathrm{ALI}$ indicates that coherence improvements
serve useful outcomes rather than trivial synchrony.
\end{definition}

\begin{corollary}[Parsimony vs.\ power confounds]
If $\mathrm{ALI}$ is high and the dissipation penalty decreases while $\mathrm{TCI}$ rises,
then power–only confounds are ruled out (cf.\ amplitude–vs–phase controls).
\end{corollary}

%----------------------------
\subsection{Neural and AI realizations}
\label{subsec:dve-tokens}

\paragraph{Dendritic vector embeddings (DVE).}
Let segment vectors $v_i\!\in\!\mathbb{R}^d$ gate an input embedding $x_t$ with
$a_i(t)=\sigma(v_i\!\cdot\!x_t-\theta_i)$. Ensembles of such gates implement
content–addressable writes/reads into the coherence field; learning $\Delta v_i$
that raises \(\mathrm{TCI}\) while lowering dissipation increases local Telos.

\paragraph{AI tokens as discrete render traces.}
For a generative model with state $h_t$ and next token $z_{t+1}\!\sim\!\mathrm{Cat}(\mathrm{softmax}(Wh_t))$,
token emission is a discretized render update. Telos–consistent training (e.g., structure priors,
diversity floors) yields token–phase alignments observable as pre–token phase resets in MEG/ECoG
(§\ref{subsec:tests}).

%----------------------------
\subsection{Operational indices and tests}
\label{subsec:tests}

Beyond \(\mathrm{TCI}\), MHI, and ATP (cf.\ §\ref{sec:sit-memory-arrow}), we use:

\[
\mathrm{ALI} \quad\text{(alignment)}, \qquad
\mathrm{TCI}\!\uparrow,\; \mathrm{PE}\!\downarrow,\; \mathrm{Dissipation}\!\downarrow \quad\text{(triad)}.
\]

\paragraph{Predictions (preregisterable).}
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{T\arabic*.}, leftmargin=1.3em]
\item \textbf{Alignment.} Interventions that increase \(\mathrm{TCI}\) and improve performance
raise \(\mathrm{ALI}\); amplitude–only controls do not (ALI stable).
\item \textbf{Parsimony.} Telos–consistent training lowers $\mathrm{Dissipation}$ while
\(\mathrm{TCI}\) rises; phase–decorrelation raises $\mathrm{Dissipation}$ and lowers \(\mathrm{TCI}\).
\item \textbf{Memory.} After learning, baseline MHI and ATP remain elevated (hysteresis);
reverse–phase “unlearning” reduces but does not eliminate the shift.
\item \textbf{DVE–coherence coupling.} Invasive/non–invasive readouts show that larger DVE margins
($v\!\cdot\!x$) correlate with higher \(\mathrm{TCI}\) and lower $\mathrm{Dissipation}$ at the same power.
\item \textbf{Token–phase alignment.} Pre–token phase resets (human MEG/ECoG) predict token identity
and lower prediction error independently of amplitude; in AIs, attention–head similarities mirror DVE–like gates.
\item \textbf{Cross–substrate universality.} Plants/biofilms exhibit Telos–triad effects using ionic–wave tasks:
phase decoherence degrades \(\mathrm{TCI}\)–performance coupling and raises $\mathrm{Dissipation}$.
\end{enumerate}

%----------------------------
\subsection{Minimal methods (summary)}
\label{subsec:methods-summary}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=1.3em]
\item Compute coherence matrices \(C^{(\omega)}\), PGD/PGE, and $J_{\mathrm{coh}}$ as in §\ref{sec:metrics}.
\item Estimate $\rho(\mathbf{r},t)$ (event–rate proxies); form \(\mathrm{Dissipation}(t)\), MHI, ATP.
\item Define \(\mathrm{Valence}(t)\) for the task (accuracy, reward rate, front velocity).
\item Build null ensembles (phase–scrambled / time–shifted / decoupled); standardize components (§3).
\item Report triad and \(\mathrm{ALI}\); run amplitude–vs–phase dissociations and decoupling controls.
\end{enumerate}

%----------------------------
\subsection{Falsifiers and ablations}
\label{subsec:falsifiers}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.2em]
\item \textbf{F1 (Alignment failure).} Robust performance gains with flat/negative \(\mathrm{ALI}\) at matched power.
\item \textbf{F2 (Parsimony failure).} Interventions that raise \(\mathrm{TCI}\) but increase $\mathrm{Dissipation}$ and do not lower \(\mathrm{PE}\).
\item \textbf{F3 (Memory parity).} No MHI/ATP asymmetry between forward vs.\ reversed schedules under phase–specific perturbations.
\item \textbf{Ablations.} Leave–one–term–out Telos: removing the dissipation term collapses parsimony; removing \(\mathrm{Valence}\)
admits trivial synchrony; removing \(\mathrm{TCI}\) decouples coherence from prediction.
\end{itemize}

%----------------------------
\subsection{Relation to memory and arrow; scope}
\label{subsec:relation-scope}

Telos selects trajectories that \emph{use} the arrow of time (ATP $\!\uparrow$) to accumulate useful structure (MHI $\!\uparrow$)
with minimal coherent work. This section is \emph{mechanistic and empirical} (not metaphysical):
it neither presumes nor requires any commitment beyond TCNR and the SIT memory/arrow in §\ref{sec:sit-memory-arrow}.
For a metaphysical extension (code–level policy acting through local renderers), see the companion paper’s
embedded–agency discussion.

\paragraph{Ethics and reporting.}
As in §5, report safety limits for perturbations, preregistered endpoints, and all nulls; Telos is \emph{not}
a measure of personhood or moral value, only a task–relative value functional coupling TCNR indices to outcomes.
```
